# White-Label & Reseller Businesses: 1324 Ideas

[← back to index](README.md) · 1324 ideas · 86 categories

Businesses where you sell someone else's product, service or inventory under your own brand, plus open-source software that could run the operation (every repo linked here was checked against GitHub). Supply sources are starting points for research. Check current partner terms yourself. Categories marked *license required* (insurance, lending, travel, health, etc.) need permits in most countries.

**Startup cost:** Low (980) · Medium (303) · High (41)

## Activities & Tickets

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Cost |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| City-pass style attraction bundle under own brand | Attraction admission tickets | e.g. Tiqets, GetYourGuide supplier API, attractions | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Bundle margin 10-20% | Medium |
| Theme-park discount ticket reseller | Theme park tickets | e.g. authorized ticket wholesalers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Wholesale spread 5-12% | Low |
| Hotel concierge activity storefront | Local tours and experiences | e.g. Viator, GetYourGuide affiliate | [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Commission 8-12% shared with hotel | Low |
| Kids' birthday-party experience booking | Party venues and entertainers | e.g. trampoline parks, entertainers | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Booking commission 15% | Low |
| Adventure-sports booking marketplace | Skydiving, rafting, bungee slots | e.g. adventure operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [alextselegidis/easyappointments](https://github.com/alextselegidis/easyappointments) | Commission 15-20% | Low |
| Museum and gallery ticket resale for tour groups | Group admission tickets | e.g. museums group sales | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [frappe/erpnext](https://github.com/frappe/erpnext) | Group margin per head | Low |
| Experience-gift voucher company | Redeemable experiences | e.g. spas, driving tracks, restaurants | [saleor/saleor](https://github.com/saleor/saleor), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Voucher markup plus breakage | Medium |
| Corporate team-building activity broker | Escape rooms and workshops | e.g. local activity vendors | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Margin 20-30% per event | Low |
| Cruise-port shore activity marketplace | Port experiences | e.g. local tour operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Commission 20% | Low |
| Broadway and West End theatre package reseller | Show tickets plus dinner | e.g. authorized ticket agencies, restaurants | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Package markup | Low |
| Golf tee-time reseller | Tee times | e.g. golf courses, tee-time wholesalers | [alextselegidis/easyappointments](https://github.com/alextselegidis/easyappointments), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Booking fee per round | Low |
| Wine-tasting tour booking site | Winery tours | e.g. wineries, tour operators | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [WordPress/wordpress-develop](https://github.com/WordPress/wordpress-develop) | Commission 15-20% | Low |
| Cooking-class marketplace in tourist cities | Cooking classes | e.g. local chefs, cooking schools | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Commission 20% | Low |
| Fishing charter booking site | Charter boat trips | e.g. charter captains | [alextselegidis/easyappointments](https://github.com/alextselegidis/easyappointments), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Commission 10-15% | Low |
| White-label activities widget for travel agencies | Global activity inventory | e.g. Viator, Musement, Hotelbeds activities | [directus/directus](https://github.com/directus/directus), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | SaaS fee plus margin share | Medium |
| Stadium tour and sports experience reseller | Stadium tours | e.g. clubs, official ticket partners | [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Commission per ticket | Low |

## Airport Transfers

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Cost |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Branded airport transfer booking site for one city | Private and shared transfers | e.g. Mozio, HolidayTaxis, local taxi firms | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Markup 15-25% per transfer | Low |
| White-label transfer widget for hotels | Hotel guest transfers | e.g. local chauffeur companies | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Revenue share with hotels | Low |
| Meet-and-greet VIP fast-track service | Airport VIP assist services | e.g. airport VIP lounges, fast-track providers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [chatwoot/chatwoot](https://github.com/chatwoot/chatwoot) | Per-passenger margin | Low |
| Cruise-port shuttle reseller | Port-to-airport shuttles | e.g. coach operators | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [pretix/pretix](https://github.com/pretix/pretix) | Per-seat markup | Low |
| Ski-resort transfer bookings | Mountain shuttle seats | e.g. Alpine transfer operators | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Commission 10-20% | Low |
| Corporate ground-transport account management | Chauffeured rides | e.g. limo affiliates, black-car networks | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [invoiceninja/invoiceninja](https://github.com/invoiceninja/invoiceninja) | Monthly invoicing margin | Medium |
| Wheelchair-accessible transfer booking | Accessible vans | e.g. accessible transport firms | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [zammad/zammad](https://github.com/zammad/zammad) | Booking fee per trip | Low |
| Group-event shuttle coordination | Event shuttle buses | e.g. coach and minibus operators | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [VROOM-Project/vroom](https://github.com/VROOM-Project/vroom) | Per-event coordination fee | Low |
| Helicopter airport transfer reseller | Heli shuttle seats | e.g. helicopter operators | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Commission 10% | Low |
| Pet-travel airport transport service | Pet taxi and cargo handling | e.g. pet transport firms | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [chatwoot/chatwoot](https://github.com/chatwoot/chatwoot) | Per-trip margin | Low |
| Airport parking aggregator | Off-airport parking spots | e.g. park-and-ride lots | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Commission 15-25% per booking | Low |
| Luggage-forwarding and storage reseller | Luggage shipping and storage | e.g. luggage couriers, storage networks | [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Per-bag margin | Low |
| Airline-partner transfer add-on API | Ancillary transfer inventory | e.g. transfer wholesalers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [directus/directus](https://github.com/directus/directus) | Wholesale-to-retail spread | Medium |
| Night-arrival safe-ride for students | Vetted student pickups | e.g. local licensed drivers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Per-ride fee to universities | Low |
| Train-station transfer and porter bundles | Station pickups with porters | e.g. local chauffeur firms | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Bundle markup | Low |

| Private-label kitchen gadgets on Amazon FBA | Sourced kitchen tools | e.g. Alibaba suppliers, sourcing agents | [inventree/InvenTree](https://github.com/inventree/InvenTree), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Product margin 25-40% after fees | Medium |
| Private-label yoga mats and props | Fitness accessories | e.g. Chinese and Indian manufacturers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Margin after FBA fees | Medium |

| Walmart Marketplace private label store | Home goods | e.g. Vietnam and India manufacturers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Product margin | Medium |
| Etsy-style handmade-look home decor | Artisan-made decor | e.g. artisan workshops abroad | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo) | Product margin | Low |
| Phone accessories private label | Cases and chargers | e.g. Shenzhen OEMs | [inventree/InvenTree](https://github.com/inventree/InvenTree), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Margin 40% | Medium |
| Garden tools and planters brand | Garden products | e.g. OEM suppliers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Product margin | Medium |
| Camping gear private label | Outdoor equipment | e.g. OEM factories | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [inventree/InvenTree](https://github.com/inventree/InvenTree) | Product margin | Medium |
| Office ergonomic accessories | Laptop stands, footrests | e.g. OEM suppliers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Product margin | Medium |
| Brand-acquisition roll-up of small FBA brands | Acquired private-label brands | e.g. FBA brand marketplaces | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [apache/superset](https://github.com/apache/superset) | Portfolio profit | High |
| TikTok Shop private-label beauty tools | Beauty gadgets | e.g. OEM suppliers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [gitroomhq/postiz-app](https://github.com/gitroomhq/postiz-app) | Margin plus affiliate commissions | Medium |
| Marketplace listing management agency | Listing optimization service | e.g. VAs, designers | [makeplane/plane](https://github.com/makeplane/plane), [metabase/metabase](https://github.com/metabase/metabase) | Monthly retainer | Low |
| Sourcing-agent service for new sellers | Factory sourcing and QC | e.g. inspection firms | [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty), [inventree/InvenTree](https://github.com/inventree/InvenTree) | 5-10% of order value | Low |
| Amazon wholesale-to-EU expansion service | Pan-EU fulfillment setup | e.g. EU 3PLs, VAT agents | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio) | Setup fee plus percentage | Low |

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Cost |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| White-label app development for marketing agencies | Mobile app builds | e.g. offshore dev studios | [appwrite/appwrite](https://github.com/appwrite/appwrite), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Resell at 1.5-2.5x dev cost | Low |
| Branded mobile ordering apps for restaurants | Template ordering apps | e.g. app template vendors | [appwrite/appwrite](https://github.com/appwrite/appwrite), [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa) | Setup plus monthly fee | Low |
| Church and community apps | Template community apps | e.g. app template studios | [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase), [discourse/discourse](https://github.com/discourse/discourse) | Monthly subscription | Low |
| MVP-development for non-technical founders | Offshore dev team sprints | e.g. dev studios | [makeplane/plane](https://github.com/makeplane/plane), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Fixed-price markup | Low |
| Internal-tools development for SMEs | Low-code internal apps | e.g. low-code builders | [ToolJet/ToolJet](https://github.com/ToolJet/ToolJet), [appsmithorg/appsmith](https://github.com/appsmithorg/appsmith) | Project plus retainer | Low |
| Shopify app white-label studio | E-commerce plugins | e.g. plugin developers | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-app license | Low |
| AI-feature integration for SaaS companies | LLM feature builds | e.g. AI dev freelancers | [langgenius/dify](https://github.com/langgenius/dify), [FlowiseAI/Flowise](https://github.com/FlowiseAI/Flowise) | Project fee | Low |
| QA and test-automation outsourcing | QA engineers | e.g. offshore QA firms | [makeplane/plane](https://github.com/makeplane/plane), [louislam/uptime-kuma](https://github.com/louislam/uptime-kuma) | Hourly markup | Low |
| Event apps for conferences | Template event apps | e.g. event app platforms | [pretalx/pretalx](https://github.com/pretalx/pretalx), [indico/indico](https://github.com/indico/indico) | Per-event license | Low |
| Gym and studio branded member apps | Template fitness apps | e.g. app templates | [wger-project/wger](https://github.com/wger-project/wger), [appwrite/appwrite](https://github.com/appwrite/appwrite) | Monthly per-studio | Low |
| White-label marketplace app builds | Two-sided marketplace templates | e.g. dev studios | [medusajs/medusa](https://github.com/medusajs/medusa), [supabase/supabase](https://github.com/supabase/supabase) | Project fee | Medium |
| Legacy app modernization subcontracting | Code migration teams | e.g. nearshore teams | [makeplane/plane](https://github.com/makeplane/plane), [coollabsio/coolify](https://github.com/coollabsio/coolify) | Hourly markup | Low |
| Booking-app builds for local services | Booking app templates | e.g. scheduler engines | [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com), [appwrite/appwrite](https://github.com/appwrite/appwrite) | Setup plus monthly | Low |
| IoT dashboard development for manufacturers | IoT dashboards | e.g. IoT platforms | [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard), [grafana/grafana](https://github.com/grafana/grafana) | Project fee | Medium |
| DevOps-as-a-service for agencies | CI/CD and hosting setup | e.g. DevOps freelancers | [coollabsio/coolify](https://github.com/coollabsio/coolify), [dokku/dokku](https://github.com/dokku/dokku) | Monthly retainer | Low |

## Background Checks & Screening

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Cost |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Tenant-screening reseller for landlords | Credit and eviction reports | e.g. consumer reporting agencies | [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm), [docusealco/docuseal](https://github.com/docusealco/docuseal) | Markup per report (FCRA compliance) | Low |
| Employment background check for SMEs | Criminal and employment verifications | e.g. CRA wholesale APIs | [opencats/OpenCATS](https://github.com/opencats/OpenCATS), [n8n-io/n8n](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n) | Per-check markup (FCRA) | Low |
| Drug-testing program for trucking fleets | DOT drug and alcohol tests | e.g. labs, collection sites, TPAs | [frappe/hrms](https://github.com/frappe/hrms), [calcom/cal.com](https://github.com/calcom/cal.com) | Per-test margin (DOT C/TPA rules) | Low |

| Business idea | What you resell | Supply sources | Open-source stack | Revenue model | Cost |

| Air-cargo GSA for small airlines | Cargo capacity sales | e.g. airline cargo departments | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [twentyhq/twenty](https://github.com/twentyhq/twenty) | Commission on cargo revenue | Medium |
| Produce and reefer freight specialist | Refrigerated truckloads | e.g. reefer carriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [thingsboard/thingsboard](https://github.com/thingsboard/thingsboard) | Margin per load (FMCSA) | Medium |
| Car-shipping broker | Auto transport | e.g. car carriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [krayin/laravel-crm](https://github.com/krayin/laravel-crm) | $150-300 per vehicle (FMCSA) | Low |
| Heavy-haul and project cargo broker | Oversize loads | e.g. specialized carriers | [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase), [nextcloud/server](https://github.com/nextcloud/server) | Margin per move | Medium |
| Intermodal rail drayage broker | Rail plus dray | e.g. IMCs, dray carriers | [odoo/odoo](https://github.com/odoo/odoo), [fleetbase/fleetbase](https://github.com/fleetbase/fleetbase) | Margin per container | Medium |
| Digital freight-quote widget for e-commerce | Instant freight quotes | e.g. rate APIs | [karrioapi/karrio](https://github.com/karrioapi/karrio), [directus/directus](https://github.com/directus/directus) | Per-shipment fee | Low |
